# Graph Report - thingtime-claude-catalog-workflow  (2026-10-18)

## Corpus Check
- 3804 files · ~9,904,740 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.
- Unclassified: 82 file(s) not represented in the graph (top: (none) 28, .css 15, .entitlements 8)

## Summary
- 56541 nodes · 159509 edges · 1179 communities (1092 shown, 87 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 96% EXTRACTED · 4% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 5587 edges (avg confidence: 0.88)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `67001f1b`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
